- **measure.cs**: Jak rozmístit body po zadané délce v AutoCADu příkazem DĚLM (MEASURE, zkratka DM): body nebo bloky od bližšího konce úsečky, oblouku či křivky. Vyzkoušejte. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.cs.md))
- **measure.de**: Wie MESSEN (MEASURE, Alias ME) in AutoCAD Punkte in festem Abstand auf einem Objekt setzt: Länge, Block, Anfangsende. Zum Testen im Browser. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.de.md))
- **measure.es**: Cómo funciona GRADUA (MEASURE, alias GD) de AutoCAD en español: puntos o bloques cada cierta longitud a lo largo de un objeto, desde el extremo designado. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.es.md))
- **measure.fr**: Comment fonctionne MESURER (MEASURE, alias ME) d'AutoCAD en français : des points ou des blocs à une longueur donnée le long d'un objet. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.fr.md))
- **measure.hu**: Az AutoCAD BEOSZTÁS (MEASURE, rövidítése BEO) parancsa: pontok vagy blokkok adott hosszanként vonalon, íven, körön vagy vonalláncon, a közelebbi végtől. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.hu.md))
- **measure.it**: Come funziona MISURA (MEASURE, alias MIS) di AutoCAD in italiano: punti o blocchi a una lunghezza data lungo un oggetto, dall'estremo selezionato. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.it.md))
- **measure.ja**: AutoCAD の MEASURE[メジャー](ME)の使い方を解説します。線分、円弧、円、ポリラインに沿って、選択した位置に近い端から一定間隔で点やブロックを配置します。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.ja.md))
- **measure.ko**: AutoCAD 길이 분할(MEASURE, ME) 사용법: 선, 호, 원, 폴리선을 따라 클릭한 위치에 가까운 끝에서부터 일정 길이마다 점이나 블록을 놓습니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.ko.md))
- **measure.pl**: Jak rozmieścić punkty co zadaną długość w AutoCADzie poleceniem ZMIERZ (MEASURE, skrót ZMI): punkty lub bloki od bliższego końca. Wypróbuj online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.pl.md))
- **measure.pt-BR**: Como funciona o MEASURE do AutoCAD em português, MEDIDA (alias ME): pontos ou blocos a cada comprimento ao longo de um objeto, a partir da ponta escolhida. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.pt-BR.md))
- **measure.ru**: Как разметить отрезок точками через равное расстояние в AutoCAD командой РАЗМЕТИТЬ (MEASURE, псевдоним РМ), точки или блоки от ближнего конца. Попробуйте. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.ru.md))
- **measure.zh-CN**: CAD 定距等分怎么操作：AutoCAD MEASURE（定距等分，快捷键 ME）沿直线、圆弧、圆或多段线按指定长度放置点或块，从靠近拾取处的一端开始。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/measure.zh-CN.md))

- **melina:release**: Release a new version of melina. Performs a complete release including: - Version bump in Cargo.toml - Changelog update with release notes - Git tag creation (vX.Y.Z) - GitHub Release with binaries for macOS (Intel + ARM) and Linux - Homebrew tap auto-update via GitHub Actions Usage: `/melina:release &lt;patch|minor|major&gt; [description]` - First argument: version bump type (default: patch) - Remaining arguments: short description of what changed (used in changelog) &lt;example&gt; user: "/melina:release minor add kill-swarm command" assistant: "Starting release v0.3.0..." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; user: "/… ([vinhnxv/melina](https://github.com/vinhnxv/melina/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/release.md))

([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-help.md))
- **memlin-help**: Summarize the installed `/memlin-*` commands by purpose: context and search, status/setup, project linking, sync/plans, actions, audit, and coordination. When `memlin` is available, run `memlin help` and relay its host-aware command reference verbatim. A Marketplace install alone does not provision that launcher; if it is absent, direct the user to Memlin Companion for the normal Cursor install an ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-help.md))
- **memlin-inbox**: Review the Memlin scribe inbox — list proposed decisions, memories, and skills and accept or reject them without leaving Claude Code. Usage - /memlin-inbox [accept|reject &lt;id&gt;] ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-inbox.md))
- **memlin-inbox**: Review the Memlin scribe inbox: run `memlin inbox` in the terminal. It lists proposed decisions, memories, and skills. To act on one, run `memlin inbox accept &lt;id&gt;` or `memlin inbox reject &lt;id&gt;`. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-inbox.md))
- **memlin-ingest-native-memory**: Pull this host's native auto-memory (MEMORY.md) into Memlin via the scribe dedup, so turning native memory off loses nothing. Usage - /memlin-ingest-native-memory [--dir &lt;path&gt;] ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-ingest-native-memory.md))
- **memlin-ingest-native-memory**: Pull this host's native auto-memory into Memlin: run `memlin ingest-native-memory` in the terminal. It reads Claude Code's native auto-memory index (`~/.claude/projects/&lt;repo&gt;/memory/MEMORY.md`) and runs each entry through the Memlin scribe dedup, so native learnings corroborate what Memlin already knows instead of duplicating. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-ingest-native-memory.md))
- **memlin-link**: Pin the current workspace to a specific Memlin account. Use when you switch Claude Code workspaces between accounts (e.g. employer / client / personal). ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-link.md))
- **memlin-link**: Pin this workspace to a specific Memlin account: run `memlin link` (or `memlin link &lt;account&gt;`) in the terminal. Use when switching the workspace between accounts — employer, client, or personal. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-link.md))
- **memlin-login**: Sign in to Memlin via Auth0 device flow. Opens a verification URL in your browser; the CLI receives access + refresh tokens that auto-rotate. Also installs the resolver SKILL.md. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-login.md))
- **memlin-login**: Sign in is an interactive browser device flow and cannot be completed by the agent. Memlin Companion owns the Cursor sign-in and integration lifecycle: ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-login.md))
- **memlin-manage-memory**: Let Memlin manage your memory — turns off this editor's native auto-memory so Memlin is your single store. Reversible; never deletes; --archive copies your files to a verified local backup before moving anything. Usage - /memlin-manage-memory [--status | --archive | --revert] ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-manage-memory.md))
- **memlin-manage-memory**: Let Memlin manage your memory: run `memlin manage-memory` in the terminal. Once Memlin captures and resolves your memory, the editor's built-in native auto-memory is a redundant, machine-bound silo that loads an unranked blob into context every session — this hands it to Memlin. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-manage-memory.md))
- **memlin-private**: Private or read-only mode — Memlin context still loads; private saves nothing from this session, read-only lets the team see the work but nothing changes team memory. Ends with the session. Usage - /memlin-private [on|read-only|off|status] ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-private.md))
- **memlin-private**: Memlin private mode: `/memlin-private` (on), `/memlin-private read-only`, `/memlin-private off`, `/memlin-private status`. Private: Memlin context still loads but nothing from this session is saved — no scribe capture, plan sync, activity or audit. Read-only: context loads and the team still sees the work, but nothing changes team memory — no capture and no saved memories, skills or decisions. Eit ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-private.md))
- **memlin-pull**: Pull the latest memory/skills from Memlin for the current project. One-way (server → local). ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-pull.md))
- **memlin-pull**: Pull the latest memory and skills from Memlin for this project (server → local, one-way): run `memlin pull` in the terminal. Report what changed. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-pull.md))
- **memlin-pull-plans**: Refresh ~/.claude/plans/ from Memlin. By default pulls the delta since the last sync; pass --full for a complete pull, or a plan id for a one-shot fetch. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-pull-plans.md))
- **memlin-pull-plans**: Refresh local plans from Memlin: run `memlin pull-plans` in the terminal (add `--full` for a complete pull). By default it pulls the delta since the last sync. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-pull-plans.md))
- **memlin-push**: Push all local memory/skill changes to Memlin as new versions. One-way (local → server). ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-push.md))
- **memlin-push**: Push local Memlin memory/skill changes to the server as new versions (local → server, one-way): run `memlin push` in the terminal. Report what was uploaded. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-push.md))
- **memlin-push-plan**: Upload a Claude Code plan file to Memlin as a versioned plan document. Auto-resolves the active project, attaches the optional resolver bundle, and prints the URL to review on memlin.ai. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-push-plan.md))
- **memlin-push-plan**: Upload a plan file to Memlin as a versioned plan document: run `memlin push-plan &lt;path-to-plan&gt;` in the terminal. It auto-resolves the active project and prints the review URL on memlin.ai. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-push-plan.md))
- **memlin-read-only**: Read-only mode — Memlin context still loads and your team still sees this work, but nothing changes team memory (no capture, no saved memories, skills or decisions). Ends with the session. Usage - /memlin-read-only [on|off|status] ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-read-only.md))
- **memlin-read-only**: Memlin read-only mode: `/memlin-read-only` (on), `/memlin-read-only off`, `/memlin-read-only status`. Context still loads and the team still sees this work, but nothing changes team memory — no capture and no saved memories, skills or decisions. It ends with the session. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-read-only.md))
- **memlin-remember**: Save a one-line durable memory with zero ceremony — routed through the scribe dedup as a provenance-verified user directive (active immediately, supersedes the stale fact it corrects). Usage - /memlin-remember [--team] [--skill] [--title "&lt;t&gt;"] [--type &lt;t&gt;] &lt;text&gt; ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-remember.md))
- **memlin-remember**: Save a one-line durable memory with zero ceremony: run `memlin remember &lt;text&gt;` in the terminal, passing the user's text through verbatim (e.g. `memlin remember our database is RLS; include RLS instructions when updating a stored procedure or table`). ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-remember.md))
- **memlin-revert**: Revert a memory or skill to an earlier version. Usage - /memlin-revert &lt;document-name&gt; [version-number] ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-revert.md))
- **memlin-revert**: Revert a Memlin memory or skill document to an earlier version: run `memlin revert &lt;document-name&gt; [version-number]` in the terminal. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-revert.md))
- **memlin-role**: Assign functional roles to a workspace member, or tag documents with roles. Functional roles (backend, sre, ...) let the resolver boost role-relevant memory and skills for the people who hold them. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-role.md))
- **memlin-scribe**: Run the session scribe over the current Claude Code transcript. Extracts decisions, memories, and skills established during this session and proposes them in your Memlin inbox. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-scribe.md))
- **memlin-scribe**: Run the Memlin session scribe over the current transcript: execute `memlin scribe`. It extracts decisions, memories, and skills established in this session and proposes them in your Memlin inbox. Report what was proposed. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-scribe.md))
- **memlin-status**: Show Memlin status — auth (token expiry, refresh state), account + project, routing, last sync, and pending local changes. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-status.md))
